{"forms":[{"form":"civilisation","tags":["alternative","UK"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"wilderness"},{"word":"wilds"},{"word":"anecumene"}],"examples":[{"text":"Some of the tourists in the upcountry might have embarrassed themselves if they'd been capable of having any shame, whining that they couldn't wait to get back to civilization.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Collectively, those people and places of the world considered to have a high standard of behavior and / or a high level of development.
